- Vérifié par rapport à la source / Verified against the source: 993 textes / texts, 0 différents / differ: OK (993 of the texts held here; each is compared at least every 7 days); the XML of the version each held act's Text: trailer names, read again from Legilux's file store (legilux.public.lu/filestore, 1017 reads this check, 1 s apart), rendered by the act's own builder (wave3, build_bulk, build.py), and compared with the act's files at the tip of main, git blob by git blob (the two history acts article by article, letters and digits); a rotating sweep reads every held act within 7 days (987 of 6908 this check), with the acts this check wrote (6); a text that differs is corrected by the next check's update (Kind: correction)
- Signalement / Flag `unexplained-gap`: a gap between the source and the repository with no reason. 4 laws at Legilux are not here with no reason found
- Signalement / Flag `publish-lag`: acts new or changed at the source for over 7 days are not published here yet. 1 laws new or changed at Legilux over 7 days ago are not here yet
- Relu par une seconde voie / Read again by a second route: 5 textes / texts, conformes au nouveau texte / match the new text 5, à l’ancien / the old 0, à aucun / neither 0, non lus / unread 0 (Legilux's HTML of the version each act's Text: trailer names (legilux.public.lu/filestore/.../fr/html; the check and verify read its XML), its hidden parts (struck-through text, modification markers) left out: every line of the act's files, letters and digits, found on the page: whole, or without a list number build.py writes from the list's symbol, or in parts split at colons and quotation marks, or 4 in 5 of its runs of four words (the page numbers a quoted provision's items, which build.py writes on one line without their numbers))
- Note: Legilux lists 9,277 laws and the Constitution: 8,077 in force or not yet in force (the census), 1,200 no longer in force (435 in this repository's history, 765 listed in data/actes/loi.csv, 0 neither); not counted as gaps (README: What it does not show): 874 laws in force Legilux has only as scanned Mémorial pages (no XML) and 270 laws in force about named private persons (adopted since 1926), each listed in data/actes/loi.csv with metadata and link, not as text; Legilux's 142,769 acts of other kinds (43,896 Mémorial B publications, 33,396 communal regulations, 15,404 règlements grand-ducaux, 12,785 arrêtés ministériels, ...) are outside this corpus's text (corpus.json: the Constitution and the laws); the repository lists part of them with metadata and link in data/actes/ (PLAN.md); not compared in this check

- Vérifié par rapport à la source / Verified against the source: 994 textes / texts, 0 différents / differ: OK (994 of the texts held here; each is compared at least every 7 days); the XML of the version each held act's Text: trailer names, read again from Legilux's file store (legilux.public.lu/filestore, 1015 reads this check, 1 s apart), rendered by the act's own builder (wave3, build_bulk, build.py), and compared with the act's files at the tip of main, git blob by git blob (the two history acts article by article, letters and digits); a rotating sweep reads every held act within 7 days (987 of 6903 this check), with the acts this check wrote (7); a text that differs is corrected by the next check's update (Kind: correction)
- Signalement / Flag `unexplained-gap`: a gap between the source and the repository with no reason. 4 laws at Legilux are not here with no reason found
- Signalement / Flag `publish-lag`: acts new or changed at the source for over 7 days are not published here yet. 1 laws new or changed at Legilux over 7 days ago are not here yet
- Relu par une seconde voie / Read again by a second route: 5 textes / texts, conformes au nouveau texte / match the new text 5, à l’ancien / the old 0, à aucun / neither 0, non lus / unread 0 (Legilux's HTML of the version each act's Text: trailer names (legilux.public.lu/filestore/.../fr/html; the check and verify read its XML), its hidden parts (struck-through text, modification markers) left out: every line of the act's files, letters and digits, found on the page: whole, or without a list number build.py writes from the list's symbol, or in parts split at colons and quotation marks, or 4 in 5 of its runs of four words (the page numbers a quoted provision's items, which build.py writes on one line without their numbers))
- Note: Legilux lists 9,277 laws and the Constitution: 8,077 in force or not yet in force (the census), 1,200 no longer in force (435 in this repository's history, 765 listed in data/actes/loi.csv, 0 neither); not counted as gaps (README: What it does not show): 886 laws in force Legilux has only as scanned Mémorial pages (no XML) and 266 laws in force about named private persons (adopted since 1926), each listed in data/actes/loi.csv with metadata and link, not as text; Legilux's 142,704 acts of other kinds (43,834 Mémorial B publications, 33,396 communal regulations, 15,404 règlements grand-ducaux, 12,782 arrêtés ministériels, ...) are outside this corpus's text (corpus.json: the Constitution and the laws); the repository lists part of them with metadata and link in data/actes/ (PLAN.md); not compared in this check

- Signalement / Flag `unexplained-gap`: a gap between the source and the repository with no reason. 2 laws at Legilux are not here with no reason found
- Signalement / Flag `audit-disagrees`: a second route disagrees with the text published. 1 of 5 read again by a second route disagree
- Relu par une seconde voie / Read again by a second route: 5 textes / texts, conformes au nouveau texte / match the new text 4, à l’ancien / the old 0, à aucun / neither 1, non lus / unread 0 (Legilux's HTML of the version each act's Text: trailer names (legilux.public.lu/filestore/.../fr/html; the check and verify read its XML), its hidden parts (struck-through text, modification markers) left out: every line of the act's files, letters and digits, found on the page: whole, or without a list number build.py writes from the list's symbol, or in parts split at colons and quotation marks, or 4 in 5 of its runs of four words (the page numbers a quoted provision's items, which build.py writes on one line without their numbers))
- Note: Legilux lists 9,272 laws and the Constitution: 8,072 in force or not yet in force (the census), 1,200 no longer in force (435 in this repository's history, 765 listed in data/actes/loi.csv, 0 neither); not counted as gaps (README: What it does not show): 897 laws in force Legilux has only as scanned Mémorial pages (no XML) and 266 laws in force about named private persons (adopted since 1926), each listed in data/actes/loi.csv with metadata and link, not as text; Legilux's 142,683 acts of other kinds (43,819 Mémorial B publications, 33,396 communal regulations, 15,404 règlements grand-ducaux, 12,780 arrêtés ministériels, ...) are outside this corpus's text (corpus.json: the Constitution and the laws); the repository lists part of them with metadata and link in data/actes/ (PLAN.md); not compared in this check
